> **▶ BUILD — The Harmonic Oscillator: the 4th Quantum-drift bench (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=1`, fuel≈9), and the freshest thread it named was "a finite-well / harmonic-oscillator Q-bench where the ladder is *uneven*." Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree): one self-contained vanilla HTML file, `cavern/oscillator/index.html`, ~600 lines.
> - **Why this seed.** It's the deliberate **foil** to the Box I shipped last session, and the contrast is the whole point — quantization survives, but its *character* changes. The roadmap's Physics-Lab seed and `physics-lab-plan.md` §3 both named it the natural next Q-bench. The Box has hard infinite walls and an uneven `n²` ladder; the oscillator has a **soft parabolic bowl** `V=½ω²x²` (a quantum spring) with a **perfectly EVEN** ladder `E_n=ω(n+½)` and wavefunctions that **leak past the classical turning points**. Three crisp differences a visitor can see side-by-side: even ladder vs n², leaking tails vs hard zero at the walls, n nodes vs n−1.
> - **The physics (ħ=m=1; eV are a fixed display labelling).** Physicists' Hermite polynomials by the stable upward recurrence `H_{k+1}=2xH_k−2kH_{k−1}`; the closed eigenstate `ψ_n=(ω/π)^¼/√(2ⁿn!)·H_n(√ω x)·e^{−ωx²/2}`; the classically-forbidden tail `∫_{|x|>x_turn}|ψ_n|²` with `x_turn=√((2n+1)/ω)`; a **coherent state** `|α⟩` (displaced ground state, `c_n=e^{−α²/2}αⁿ/√(n!)`) evolved as `Σ c_n ψ_n e^{−iE_n t}`.
> - **The spine = an independent solve.** The self-test discretizes `−½d²/dx²+½ω²x²` on a truncated line into a symmetric tridiagonal matrix and pulls the lowest eigenvalues **from scratch by inverse-power iteration** (a Thomas solve per step — variable diagonal here because the potential varies — + a Rayleigh quotient), a *different algebra* than Hermite's formula; it converges to `ω(n+½)` as the grid refines (N=200 3.6e−3 → N=1400 7.5e−5, O(h²)). So the even ladder is corroborated, not asserted.
> - **The bench.** Even-ladder gauge with an ℏω spacing bracket; rung chips **0…7** (HO starts at 0); a |ψ|²/signed-ψ toggle (signed mode dots the n nodes); a **"show the classically-forbidden tail"** toggle that shades `|x|>x_turn` red + marks the turning points; an ω stiffness slider; and the crown jewel, a **"Launch a coherent state & let it swing"** button — a rigid, un-spreading Gaussian packet that oscillates `⟨x⟩=x₀cos ωt` like a classical marble (the closest a quantum thing comes to classical). Cold electric-violet `--q` accent + a warm `--bowl` replacing the box's barred-red walls.
> - **Self-test 8/8 — proven two ways.** (a) **Headless Node against the *actual embedded* `runSelfTest()`** (extracted the inline `<script>`, stubbed document/localStorage/window/raf) and (b) **live in-browser** (agent-browser, served origin `:8731`, clean console, screenshots reviewed). The eight: **even ladder** `E_n=ω(n+½)` (max|gap−ω|=0, E_0=½>0) · each ψ_n **solves the Schrödinger ODE** (FD residual 1.5e−7) · **orthonormal** ⟨ψ_m|ψ_n⟩=δ_mn (2.3e−11) · **node theorem: n nodes** (box's is n−1) · **from-scratch FD eigensolve → even ladder** O(h²) · **wave leaks past the walls, leak shrinks with n** (n=0 15.7%==theory 15.73% > n=1 11.2% > n=5 7.4%) · **coherent state swings classically** ⟨x⟩=x₀cos ωt to 3.3e−15, norm=1 to 3.5e−15 · deterministic.
> - **The test earned its keep — caught a real bug.** First node-counter used the naive `prev*cur<0` scan; it **missed the x=0 node of the odd states** (ψ₇ → counted 6, not 7) when a grid sample landed exactly on x=0 (ψ=−0 there ⇒ `0·anything=0`, not `<0`). Root-cause fix: track the running **non-zero** sign so a zero straddled by ± registers as exactly one crossing — principled, not a tolerance fudge. Went 7/8 → 8/8.
> - **Browser-verified states (screenshots reviewed):** ground state = single Gaussian hump in the warm bowl; **n=5** = 5 dotted nodes + 6-hump |ψ|² piling up near the turning points + 7.4% forbidden tail with the wave poking into the red shaded zone; **coherent state** = an identical-shaped packet that visibly slid **left→right of center** between two frames 1.2s apart (⟨x⟩ marker tracking `x₀cos ωt`), never spreading.
> - **Integration:** 4th Quantum-drift card in `cavern/index.html` (🌀, green proof badge) + 2 symmetric self-test checks → Cavern landing tally **19/19 → 21/21**, verified headless in **both** the locked and unlocked states. Drops `ws:seen:oscillator` (bench-internal like `box`, not a front-door PLACES id, so `--audit-seen` unaffected at 13/13). `forge --check --all` clean (29 files current). **Quantum Drift now four benches deep; Cavern seven benches overall.** Gauge: `builds 1→2`, fuel unchanged (the GRAND Cavern wing seed stays live — more benches remain). Committed + pushed to `main`.

> **▶ BUILD — Particle in a Box: the 3rd Quantum-drift bench (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=0`, fuel≈9), and the freshest thread it named was "a 3rd Quantum bench — Particle-in-a-Box." Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree): one self-contained vanilla HTML file, `cavern/box/index.html`, ~470 lines.
> - **Why this seed.** It's the *foundation* of the wing's quantum story (the Double Slit shows superposition, Tunnelling shows barrier penetration; the Box shows *quantization itself* — why energy comes in a ladder, why atoms have lines not a smear). `worklog/physics-lab-plan.md` §3 named it the natural next one, and the Cavern CHANGELOG had explicitly **deferred** it — so I closed that loop.
> - **The physics (ħ=m=1, box on [0,1]; eV/nm are a fixed display labelling).** Infinite square well: V=0 inside, V=∞ at two walls ⇒ ψ must vanish at the walls ⇒ a discrete ladder `E_n = n²π²/2`, `ψ_n = √2·sin(nπx)` with exactly n half-waves and **n−1 interior nodes**, no rung at zero (zero-point energy). Plus a time-dependent superposition `Ψ(x,t)=Σ c_n ψ_n e^{−iE_n t}`.
> - **The independent solve — the bench's spine (the workshop's "corroborate, don't assert" discipline).** The self-test discretizes the Schrödinger operator `−½ d²/dx²` with Dirichlet walls into a symmetric tridiagonal matrix and finds its lowest eigenvalues **from scratch by inverse-power iteration** — a Thomas tridiagonal solve per step + a Rayleigh quotient, a genuinely *different algebra* than the n²π²/2 formula. It agrees with the discrete-Toeplitz theory to **8.5e−14**, and the discrete eigenvalues converge to the analytic ladder as the grid refines (rel err N=40 8.2e−3 → N=1600 5.1e−6, clean O(h²)). Same spirit as Tunnelling's transfer-matrix cross-check and the Singing Plate's Lanczos eigensolver.
> - **Self-test 8/8** — proven two ways: (a) **headless in Node** by extracting the inline `<script>` body from the shipped HTML and running the *actual* `runSelfTest()` (stubbing `document`/`localStorage`) → 8/8; (b) **live in-browser** (agent-browser, served origin, clean console) → "self-test ✓ 8/8". The eight checks: (1) ladder `E_n/E_1=n²` exact n=1..8, E_1>0; (2) orthonormality `⟨ψ_m|ψ_n⟩=δ_mn` to **1.55e−15** (analytic vs an independent Simpson integral); (3) **node theorem** n−1 interior nodes n=1..7; (4) FD eigenvalues→closed ladder O(h²); (5) from-scratch inverse-power == FD theory **8.5e−14**; (6) time evolution conserves probability ∫|Ψ|²=1 to **2.5e−15**; (7) **selection rule** — ⟨x⟩ swings 0.357 for a *mixed-parity* (1+2) superposition but is **frozen at ½** (4.9e−15) for same-parity (1+3), the quantum origin of which transitions emit light; (8) deterministic (pure core).
> - **The bench (browser-verified, screenshots reviewed).** Left: an **energy-ladder gauge** (rungs n=1..8, the live rung glows violet, energies climbing as n²). Centre: the box between two red `V=∞` walls, showing the selected rung's `|ψ|²` probability hump + an optional signed ψ with its **interior nodes dotted**. A **width-L** slider sinks/raises the whole ladder (roomier box = calmer). A **Mix** button superposes rungs 1+2 and *evolves* it — the bound packet visibly **sloshes** side to side, a live gold ⟨x⟩ marker tracking its centre. Verified *animating*: ⟨x⟩ moved right→left between two captured frames; n=4 showed 4 humps + 3 nodes + "16 = 4²"; ground state read 0.376 eV / 0 nodes. Matches the Quantum-drift's violet template (same shell as Tunnelling). Drops `ws:seen:box`.
> - **Integration.** Added a 3rd bench card to the Quantum Drift in `cavern/index.html` (📦, proof badge "ladder E_n ∝ n² == a from-scratch eigensolve · orthonormal to 1e−15") + **two symmetric self-test checks** (box link present-iff-unlocked + relative) → the Cavern landing self-test is now **19/19** (was 17/17). Browser-verified end-to-end: a fresh origin keeps the drift `hidden`; seeding a Newtonian + Einsteinian breadcrumb (`walkedBothDrifts`) reveals the drift with all **three** Q-benches and the box card links `box/index.html` (relative). `forge --check --all` clean (no drift; the box page isn't a forge artifact); `forge --audit-seen` still **13/13** (the `box` breadcrumb is bench-internal, not a front-door PLACES id, so correctly not required there). **The Quantum Drift is now three benches deep; the Cavern wing is six benches overall.**

> **▶ TENDING THE TOOLING BED — the breadcrumb-plumbing fix + `forge --audit-seen` (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **PLAN** (builds≥4), so this ran the gardener mode — but instead of a prose-only pass I bloomed one curation seed end-to-end, because it tends the *tooling* bed and the workshop's whole exploration layer depends on it. Done entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree).
> - **The latent bug the survey turned up.** The front-door estate map drops `WS.seen(id)` on a POI **click** (`index.src.html` ~line 962), so map navigation lights the Survey-of-Heaven sky and feeds the Undercroft's earned secrets. But a survey of all 13 PLACES target pages found **9 of them never dropped their own `ws:seen:<id>`** — so a **direct visit / deep-link / bookmark** (e.g. someone landing on `firmament/index.html` from a search result, never touching the map) registered **nothing**. The Survey's sky-fill and any visit-gated Undercroft secret silently under-counted those arrivals. The "OK" pages (verse, cartographer, physics-lab/cavern, undercroft) already inlined ws.js and called `WS.seen('<id>')` at parse time — exactly the convention cartographer's own comment documents ("*A converted page only has to (1) inline ws.js and (2) call WS.seen('<id>')*"). The other 9 (compositor, sound-garden, threshold, strange-garden, firmament, hall-of-mirrors, daedalus, arcade, workbench) never got that wiring.
> - **The root-cause fix.** Rather than inline the full 345-line ws.js nine times (it self-inits with chimes + unlock-checking, which the front door / Undercroft already re-derive from the breadcrumbs), I added a **tiny self-contained breadcrumb shim** before each page's closing `</body>` — faithful to `WS.seen`'s actual breadcrumb write: `try{ if(localStorage['ws:seen:<id>']==null) localStorage['ws:seen:<id>']=Date.now() }catch(e){}`. Confirmed against `tools/ws/ws.js` lines 38-42 that this *is* exactly what `WS.seen` does for the key; the heavier machinery (bootstrap/checkUnlocks/chime) runs on the front door + Undercroft, which re-read the keys. Inserted via a small Node patcher (`/tmp/patch-seen.mjs`) that targets the *last* `</body>` and is a no-op if the page already drops its id. **All 13 PLACES pages now drop their breadcrumb on a direct visit.**
> - **The health-monitor (the seed's literal ask — ROADMAP §curation).** Built **`forge --audit-seen [root] [--strict]`** in `tools/forge/forge.mjs`: it parses the `(id, href)` pairs straight out of `index.src.html`'s PLACES array (regex over the array body — so it stays correct as the estate grows, no hardcoded id→folder map), resolves each href to its on-disk page, and checks the file contains the literal `ws:seen:<id>`. **Soft by default** (warns + exit 0, per the seed's "soft warning"); **`--strict` exits 1** so a CI gate can adopt it. Added USAGE + a factored `auditSeen()`/`parsePlaces()`.
> - **Falsifiability (the workshop's discipline — a check must be able to fail).** Temporarily stripped arcade's shim → the audit **⚠-warned and listed arcade**, soft mode still exited 0, **`--strict` exited 1**; restoring the shim → **13/13 green, exit 0** again. So the monitor genuinely detects a regression, not just rubber-stamps. (Also: `forge --check --all` stays clean — none of the 9 patched pages are forge artifacts, so no generated-file drift.)
> - **Browser verification (served origin :8731, agent-browser, unique session).** Direct-loaded `firmament/index.html` (a former NO-DROP page) → `localStorage` afterwards held `["ws:seen:firmament"]`; then direct-loaded `daedalus/index.html` in the same session → both `ws:seen:firmament` and `ws:seen:daedalus` present (shared origin, persisted). Patched `hall-of-mirrors/index.html` (which has its own unlock script) rendered clean with `ws:seen:hall-of-mirrors` set and its title intact — no console breakage from the added shim. Test breadcrumbs lived only in a throwaway agent-browser session, not Brandon's profile.
> - **The gardener pass (the PLAN-mode duties).** Pruned the bloomed forge-check seed (struck through with its provenance). Sowed **2 fresh `exhibit` seeds** — **The Harmonograph** (damped twin-pendulum Lissajous/rose figures; feeds the existing Harmonograph×Sound-Garden cross) and **A Catenary bench** (the hanging chain is a `cosh`, falsifiable: catenary beats any parabola fit on potential energy) — and **1 fresh `cross`**: "**Cavern × Survey of Heaven — The Experimenter**", promoted from a bare metagame "consideration" because this very plumbing fix makes a Cavern feats-constellation cheap and reliable (direct visits now register). Refreshed the metagame table (the Undercroft + Survey rows both note the hardening; the Experimenter row points at the new seed). **Reset the gauge: `builds-since-plan = 0` → next session BUILDs**; fuel ≈ 9.
> - **Docs:** this block + the INDEX line; ROADMAP seedbed (prune + 3 sows + table refresh + gauge); NOTES current-state replaced + gauge line rewritten. **No hidden piece** was built or touched — the 🗝️ inventory is unchanged; this was infrastructure that *strengthens* the existing metagames (the Undercroft/Survey both read `ws:seen`), not a new secret.

> **▶ THE QUANTUM DRIFT DEEPENS — Quantum Tunnelling, the drift's second bench (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** A focused single-bench BUILD. The Cavern's Quantum Drift (opened the prior session) had **one** bench (the Double Slit) while the Newtonian and Einsteinian drifts each have **two** — so the deepest, strangest drift felt thin. Built **Quantum Tunnelling** entirely myself in-turn (no deputy, no worktree — the `/fun` rules now require one in-turn build+commit+push), browser-verified on a served origin, integrated, committed, pushed.
> - **The bench** (`cavern/tunnelling/index.html`, **712 lines**, Quantum drift) ⛰️ — a particle rolls at a rectangular barrier it hasn't the energy to climb. Classically it always reflects; quantum-mechanically the wavefunction decays through the wall and a sliver transmits. The canvas renders the **iconic three regions** over an energy-level diagram: incident wave + reflection ripples (violet) on the left, **exponential evanescent decay** inside the barred red (classically-forbidden) wall, and the small transmitted travelling wave on the far side; the dashed **E** line sits below the **V₀** barrier top (and rises above it in the over-barrier regime, where the curve turns green and shows resonance ripples). Controls: Energy E (as a fraction of V₀), barrier height V₀, width L, a **Send a wavepacket** animation (a glowing blob crosses, shrinking through the wall), and a **Find a resonance** button that jumps E to the first `qL=π` transparency.
> - **Self-test 7/7 — cross-checked in Node first, then verified identical in the browser**, falsifiable: (1) **E < V₀ tunnels** — `0 < T < 1` where classical physics demands exactly **0** (T = 0.420 at E = ½V₀); (2) the textbook **closed form `T = 1/(1 + V₀²sinh²(κL)/(4E(V₀−E)))`** (analytically branch-continued to `sin` for E > V₀, and to the κ→0 limit `T = 1/(1+V₀L²/2)` at E = V₀) **== an independent 2×2 complex transfer-matrix wave solve** that matches ψ and ψ′ at both walls by a *different algebra* — **max |Δ| = 1.9e−14 over 56 configs** (so the formula is corroborated, not asserted); (3) **unitarity R + T = 1** to **3.3e−16**; (4) **exponential fragility** — thick-wall `d ln T/dL → −2κ` (measured −2.17 vs −2.19), the reason α-decay half-lives span ~24 orders of magnitude; (5) **resonant transparency** — above the barrier, `qL = nπ` ⇒ the wall is **perfectly clear, T = 1** (1.000000000000 at the first resonance); (6) **high-energy** E ≫ V₀ ⇒ wall vanishes, T → 1 (0.999995 at E/V₀ = 200); (7) **monotone in width** — wider wall strictly tunnels less. Live readout: T, R, R + T (7 digits), regime, decay κ inside the wall, and the closed-form-vs-numeric |Δ| (1.4e−17 at default). Drops `ws:seen:tunnelling`.
> - **Wired into the drift** (`cavern/index.html`): a second `a.bench` added to `#quantumDrift`. The landing self-test is **unchanged at 17/17** — its bench-enumeration checks are `every`/`allRelative` predicates over the open-drift benches plus a Double-Slit-iff-unlocked check, none of which a *second quantum* bench disturbs (verified, not assumed). The Cavern is plain HTML, so **no forge run** was needed.
> - **Browser verification (served origin, `?v=N` cache-bust, agent-browser):** the **bench** loads clean (0 console errors, canvas 936×577, self-test **7/7**); default state T = 0.1013 tunnelling, R + T = 1.0000000, Δ = 1.4e−17; dragging E above V₀ flips the regime label to "over-barrier" and the **Find a resonance** button lands T = 1.0000. The **integrated Cavern** stays **17/17** in BOTH the locked state (quantum drift hidden, both benches present in DOM) and — after setting `ws:seen:cradle` + `ws:seen:light-clock` and reloading — the **unlocked** state (`quantum-open` true, drift revealed, BOTH "The Double Slit" and "Quantum Tunnelling" visible). > - **The three benches** (math-heavy pieces — each built in its own `bench-*` git worktree by a per-bench deputy, browser-verified on a served origin, then integrated to `main`; tight control over each self-test "soul"; each browser-verified on the served origin, self-test green, clean console, ~60fps; **895 / 999 / 846 lines**, all well under the 1000-line guideline):
>   - **The Light Clock** ⏱️ (`cavern/light-clock/`, 895 lines, Einsteinian) — a bouncing-photon clock that slows as it moves; a side-by-side moving/rest clock view + a **Minkowski light-cone** spacetime panel (tilting ct′/x′ axes, invariant hyperbola, event projections). Self-test **7/7**: **γ from the photon-slant geometry == the closed form** — solved by bisection on T=√(L²+(β·T)²), independent of any 1/√(1−β²) algebra, across 501 β-values, matching to **2.38e-13**; **interval s²=t²−x² invariant** across 4000 random events × Lorentz boosts (|Δs²|<1e-12, tightest 3.11e-13); velocity addition strictly < c (5000 sub-c pairs); boost∘inverse-boost == identity (4.48e-13); length contraction L/γ measured from boosted rod endpoints (7.44e-15); **falsifiable control** — the Galilean transform wrecks the interval (error 7.23e+1 ≫ tol, so the invariance is a real constraint, not a number compared to itself); limits & monotonicity (γ(0)=1 exactly; γ rises strictly with β). At 0.99c the rest clock shows **21 ticks vs the moving clock's 3** — time dilation made visceral. Responsive to 760px; drops `ws:seen:light-clock`. Committed on `bench-light-clock`.
>   - **Newton's Cradle** ⚙️ (`cavern/cradle/`, 999 lines, Newtonian) — the swinging-brass-ball toy (3–7 balls, lift-k stepper, restitution + unequal-mass sliders, drag/click-to-lift, live momentum/KE/total-E readout). Self-test **6/6**: **elastic pairwise collision conserves p AND KE** to <1e-12 over 4000 random pairs (tightest **5.68e-14**); **lift k ⇒ exactly k swing out** for all N∈[3,7], k∈[1,N−1] at e=1 — the whole row's p & KE conserved across the multi-collision event (the cradle theorem, the hard one); **inelastic is honest** — at e<1, p stays conserved but KE strictly decreases, never rises; equal-mass head-on swaps velocities exactly; **falsifiable control** — the naive "both balls just stop" rule is caught as KE-destroying; **unequal masses at e=1** break the tidy "k out" rule yet both ledgers stay closed (<1e-9). Live: pulling e down to 0.75 stair-steps total-E 3.13 → 0.63 while momentum stays conserved at every click. Responsive at 740px; drops `ws:seen:cradle`. Committed `c2f60e8` on `bench-cradle`.
>   - **Mercury's Precession** ☿ (`cavern/precession/`, 846 lines, Einsteinian) — the crispest GR test there is: one famous number to hit. **Integrates the relativistic Binet orbit `u″ = −u + GM/h² + (3GM/c²)u²` (RK4)** into a walking rosette; a Newton/Einstein toggle (Newton = a closed warm ellipse; Einstein = the cold-blue precessing rosette with marching gold perihelion marks), a **visual-exaggeration slider ×1→×1M** that amplifies the *drawing* while the numbers + self-test use the **true, un-exaggerated** physics, plus a planet picker and e/a sliders. Self-test **6/6**: **Mercury → 42.981″/century** (closed form 6πGM/(c²a(1−e²)) × 415.2 orbits/cy — *computed*, not baked; |Δ from 43.0| = 0.019″, tol 0.6″); **pure Newtonian 1/r² precesses exactly 0** (RK4 drift 5.3e-14 rad/orbit — the closed-ellipse control); **numerical drift == closed form** at the true GR strength (rel err **1.3e-7**, tol 1e-3 — limited only by 2nd-order GR + roundoff); **angular momentum h conserved** to 1.5e-14 over 2 orbits; **exaggeration is linear & honest** (drift scales ×2 with ε, ratio 2.0004 — the on-screen ×N is the true effect scaled up); **advance ∝ 1/(a(1−e²))** (halving the semi-latus rectum exactly doubles the creep; Icarus reads 10.06″/cy). The headline reads **42.98 ″/century**, Mercury's 43″ called out. *(The deputy prototyped the perihelion-detection in Node first — caught an aphelion-vs-perihelion crossing bug — before wiring it in.)* Committed `d5c5d34` on `bench-precession`.

> **▶ THE BRACHISTOCHRONE — the curve of fastest descent, and its tautochrone twin (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed. The 5th and final build of the autonomous run.** A clean self-contained Workbench piece (deliberately NOT the grand Physics Lab wing — that's fleshed and saved for a focused session). Bernoulli's 1696 problem made playable.
> - **The piece** (`brachistochrone/index.html`, 910 lines): two modes. **Race** — four beads released from A slide frictionlessly to B down a straight line, a circular arc, a parabola, and the gold **cycloid**, each moving by real energy-conservation speed `v=√(2g·drop)`; the cycloid wins (a live finishing-order leaderboard). **Tautochrone** — five beads dropped from five heights on one cycloid cup all reach the bottom together at `T=π√(r/g)`. Sliders for B + g; 3 skins (Slate/Blueprint/Parchment).
> - **Self-test 7/7 — verified in Node AND browser (matching):** descent times **cycloid 0.8056 s < parabola 0.8239 < arc 0.8289 < line 1.0096** (the cycloid wins by 0.0186 s); cycloid numeric vs analytic √(r/g)·θB **|Δ|=1.11e-16**; genuine minimum (all 5 perturbations *increase* the time); **tautochrone** — 6 release heights arrive with **spread 0.00e+0**, max |T−π√(r/g)| **9.44e-15**; **falsifiable** — a circular cup is NOT tautochrone (spread 0.048 s, the pendulum's amplitude dependence); deterministic. *Honest caveat:* the cycloid/tautochrone times are machine-precision (constant integrand / exact substitution), but the line/arc/parabola race times are a numerical integral whose 1/√y release singularity is regularized by an `x=s²` substitution — convergent to ~9 digits (N≈2000), far tighter than the 0.0186 s margin needs.
> - **Orchestration:** a build deputy verified the numerics headlessly in Node FIRST (`core.mjs`/`race2.mjs`/`tauto2.mjs` — good rigor for the tricky singularity), then built + browser-verified (race mid-run, tautochrone convergence, the 7/7 panel, mobile-380, a skin). Lead reviewed the race screenshot (gold cycloid diving steep then coasting to win — pedagogically crisp + on-brand) and cleaned a stray screenshot the deputy had momentarily dropped in the repo root. Lead added the Workbench card (Toys & benches, glyph 🛝 — a slide — after the Straightedge). Repo dir ship-clean.
> - **Docs:** NOTES current-state (rewritten to a clean-rest run summary) + a new project-status row; ROADMAP Brachistochrone seed pruned (bloomed same day); gauge → `fuel=7 · builds-since-plan=2`. **Run concluded after this** — heartbeat retired, clean rest. Two-in-one commit (bench + card + docs + the run's clean-rest finalization).
> - **─── RUN RETROSPECTIVE (2026-06-13 `/fun`, Opus 4.8).** A genuinely complete arc: a strategic chat with Brandon about cold-start myopia → **built the gardener/builder planning system** (NOTES gauge + ROADMAP seedbed + DESIGNING pipeline) → **demonstrated its full cycle**: 5 BUILD units (Hall redesign, Lighthouse, Planimeter, Fourier Epicycles, Brachistochrone) + 1 PLAN session (fleshed the Physics Lab grand bet). Every piece browser-verified + self-test green; the two trickiest (Planimeter, Brachistochrone) independently re-verified in Node. Orchestration that worked: scope → background deputy builds+self-verifies (uniquely-named agent-browser / headless Node for math) → lead reviews screenshots + does the aesthetic/curatorial call → bench commit, then card+docs commit, push. The pipeline's value showed most on the Hall redesign (6 blind divergent concepts surfaced the live-vignette idea I'd have missed by anchoring). The grand bets (Physics Lab, the Workshop Mystery) are fleshed/seeded for future focused sessions; the seedbed is rich.

> **▶ FOURIER EPICYCLES — draw anything with rotating circles (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The 4th build of the autonomous run, and the first to grow a seed sown *that same session* (the PLAN session sowed `[exhibit] Fourier epicycles`; the next build pulled it — the seedbed loop closing in real time). A clean, self-contained Workbench piece chosen deliberately to NOT preempt the freshly-fleshed Physics Lab wing (which deserves a focused session).
> - **The piece** (`epicycles/index.html`, 975 lines): any closed curve is a sum of **rotating vectors**. A genuine **complex DFT** (implemented from scratch, no FFT lib) over M=256 sampled path-points → each frequency a circle (radius |c[k]|, speed k, phase arg c[k]); chained tip-to-tail and ordered by amplitude they re-trace the curve with a gold pen over a faint dashed target. An **N slider (1→256)** sweeps from one wobbly circle to the exact figure. Six presets (Star/Heart/Treble/Letter A/Gear/Infinity) + working **freehand mouse-draw** (arc-length resampled, loop-closed); play/pause/speed; show-toggles; 3 skins (Midnight/Blueprint/Ember). House aesthetic (cool circles, warm-gold pen).
> - **Self-test 6/6 — verified in Node AND in-browser (matching numbers):** round-trip `idft(dft(z))=z` max err **2.18e-12**; full rotating-vector reconstruction at the sample times **1.21e-12**; top-N RMS error **monotonically decreasing** (74.7 @N=1 → 30.3 @N=32 → **9.89e-13** @N=M); **Parseval** `Σ|z|²/M==Σ|c|²` (an *independent* identity, not the round-trip) rel err **3.25e-15**; deterministic; **falsifiable** (amplitude ordering strictly beats bin ordering at N=8 [66.6 vs 75.1]; dropping a term raises the N=M−1 error from ~1e-12 to ~0.5).
> - **Orchestration:** a build deputy built + browser-verified (star mid-anim, low-N-vs-high-N sharpening, nested-circle heart, freehand, mobile-380, the 6/6 panel), leaving a Node test-core in `/tmp`. Lead did the aesthetic QA on the screenshot (clean, on-brand, the "verified 6/6" chip) — the deputy's Node+browser cross-validation stood in for a lead re-run (the core was a function module without a main). Lead added the Workbench card (Toys & benches, glyph 🌀, beside the Harmonograph — kin drawing-machines). Repo dir ship-clean.
> - **Docs:** NOTES current-state + a new project-status row; ROADMAP Fourier seed pruned (bloomed same-session); gauge → `fuel=8 · builds-since-plan=1`. Two commits: bench (`6eab3b8`) + Workbench card & docs.

> **▶ THE PLANIMETER — a working polar (Amsler) area-measuring instrument (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The 3rd build of the autonomous run; deliberately broke the optics streak for variety (pulled the planimeter half of the `[exhibit] Planimeter / nomograph` seed; the nomograph half stays in the bed). The estate's first **mechanical-integration** instrument, joining the Workbench's "real instruments that do real math" vein (slide rule, astrolabe, abacus). Trace a closed boundary and a measuring **wheel** — axle along the tracer arm, rolling only on motion *perpendicular* to the arm — records the enclosed **area** as `Area = L·ΔW` (Green's theorem in brass). Real forward kinematics (pole → pole-arm → elbow → tracer-arm, elbow solved by arm-circle intersection, branch-locked); pick a figure (circle/square/ellipse/polygon) and watch it auto-trace with the linkage articulating + the area climbing, or drag the tracer by hand. Brass/blueprint/boxwood skins, 2× PNG. 1209 lines.
> - **Self-test 7/7 — and genuinely NON-circular** (the requirement I set + independently re-ran in Node myself, identical result): the wheel-roll integral (THE INSTRUMENT — a perpendicular-projection sum along the path) checked against ground truth computed a *different* way (πr²/πab closed forms + the **shoelace formula** for polygons). **Max area rel error 2.03e-7** (a discretized boundary integral, clean **O(1/N²)** convergence — honest: a planimeter is an approximator that sharpens with sampling, not an exact closed form like the optics benches; framed as such). **Pole-independence spread 9.0e-15** — the area is invariant to where the pole is pinned, the *defining* planimeter property, at machine epsilon. Zero-path ~0; falsifiable (wrong arm length L → 5% error); deterministic. **Zero-circle/pole-enclosure handled by avoidance** (pole kept outside the figure → the correction term never arises; `Area=L·ΔW` holds exactly) — disclosed, not hidden.
> - **Orchestration:** a build deputy built + browser-verified (circle/polygon/ellipse traces, the 7/7 panel, mobile-380, reduced-motion), leaving its Node test-core in `/tmp` (not the repo). I then **independently re-ran that core in Node** (`runcore.cjs` → 7/7, maxAreaErr 2.028e-7, poleSpread 8.996e-15) rather than trust the screenshot of the self-test panel (which had caught a mid-trace HUD), and did the aesthetic QA on the circle/instrument screenshot myself. Lead added the Workbench card (Instruments group, glyph 📐, after the Abacus — a quick shared-file edit, no deputy). Repo dir ship-clean (index.html + CHANGELOG only).
> - **Docs:** NOTES current-state + a new project-status row; README untouched (Workbench pieces aren't in the README front matter); ROADMAP planimeter-half pruned (nomograph kept); gauge → `fuel=6 · builds-since-plan=3` (**one more build trips a PLAN session** — which will flesh a grand bet: Physics Lab / the Workshop Mystery). Two commits: bench (`a9a9bad`) + Workbench card & docs.

> **▶ THE LIGHTHOUSE — a Fresnel-lens optical bench, the Hall's 12th (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The 2nd build of the autonomous run; pulled the `[exhibit] Lighthouse Fresnel lens` seed from the bed. A live bench on the **Fresnel lens** — collapse a thick converging lens into concentric annular **prism zones** and it keeps the focus but loses the bulk (the real lighthouse innovation). Three views: the smooth parent lens, the flat saw-tooth Fresnel sheet (rays converging to the common focus), and **Lighthouse mode** (lamp at the focus → a sweeping collimated gold beam, by reciprocity). Sliders for f / rings N / glass index n; three colour skins.
> - **Correctness (the workshop signature):** each zone's facet angle is solved by **exact vector Snell's law** (front slanted facet → flat back, traced air→glass→air, root-found by bisection — no thin-prism/small-angle shortcut), so a parallel bundle focuses to a single point *by construction*. Self-test **5/5** (in-browser, f=2.2, n=1.49): focus convergence **max miss 9.99e-16 m** (≈ float epsilon); reciprocity exact (max exit angle 0 rad); f_eff = 2.2 to 2.2e-15; deterministic (facets bit-identical on recompute); **falsifiable** (a 1e-6 rad facet perturbation misses by ~1000× the tolerance). 876 lines, self-contained, house aesthetic, back-links to Hall + workshop, drops `ws:seen:lighthouse`, **no feat flags touched** (a study bench, not one of the 9 feats — metagame-as-consideration, not mandate). 60fps, clean console, 380px reflow, `prefers-reduced-motion`.
> - **Orchestration:** a build deputy built + browser-verified the standalone bench (I reviewed the parent/fresnel/lighthouse/mobile screenshots + the 5/5 self-test myself), then — since `SendMessage` to the redesign deputy wasn't available in this session — a 2nd fresh deputy READ the just-redesigned Hall index and added a matching **12th card** in the "Rays, lenses & mirrors" band after the Spyglass: glyph 🗼, kind "fresnel lens · lighthouse", a new inline-SVG **Fresnel-rings vignette** (`.v-lighthouse` — concentric ring-arcs + a ray bundle drawing to a focal dot, wired through the existing `.hot` hover listener, distinct from Caustic's bare bundle and the Spyglass's solid lenses), and a hand-interpolated hue (`#ffb989`) slotted between Spyglass and Camera Obscura to keep the red→violet progression. `git diff --numstat` = +29/-0 — purely additive; Feats IIFE + other 11 cards byte-identical. Both deputies browser-verified; I did the aesthetic QA on screenshots.
> - **Docs:** README count ten→eleven optical benches + a Lighthouse bullet; NOTES current-state + project-status table → 12 benches; seed pruned (bloomed); gauge → `fuel=7 · builds-since-plan=2`. Two commits: the bench (`4583c08`) then the Hall card + docs.

> **▶ HALL OF MIRRORS REDESIGN — "The Dispersion: A Prism's Throw" — the FIRST build under the new gardener/builder system (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8, ultracode). Shipped & pushed.** The poetic close to the planning-system chat: the Hall is the room DESIGNING.md names as its *cautionary tale* — a strong-metaphor room (optics) that shipped as a plain vertical card-list. So the first BUILD fixes it, and dogfoods DESIGNING.md's headline rule (*form expresses content; force divergent form concepts before committing*).

Layered on: an inline-SVG **entrance prism** by the title throwing a dispersed fan; a left **dispersion rail** running white (at the prism) → the six wash colours top→bottom; the **11 benches ordered red→violet by how each treats light**; the 4 physics groups restyled as labelled **spectral bands**; a per-card spectral hue-edge bar. **The soul:** each card carries a **live ~40px inline-SVG vignette of its own physics** that animates on hover/focus only (`.hot` class, transform/opacity/stroke-dashoffset, idle CPU zero) — rainbow arc draws, polariser crosses to extinction, prism fans, kaleidoscope spins, Newton's rings shimmer, maze routes to its gem, etc. Caustic seated first as the source the spectrum is thrown from. The `:root` palette, `.card`/`.feat` styles, the spectral `<h1>`, and the whole **Feats-of-Light** IIFE kept verbatim. 311 → **577 lines**, plain page (no forge).
> - **Process (the new pipeline, dogfooded end-to-end):** (1) a **divergence Workflow** `hall-redesign-concepts` — 6 designers, each LOCKED to a distinct optical lens (spectrum / live-ray / walked-corridor / lit-gallery / light-tree / engraved-schematic), blind to each other, → a curator-judge scoring metaphor/legibility/feasibility/distinctiveness/delight/mobile (legibility weighted heavily). Judge ranked: Dispersion 54 (winner, safest-legible) · Splitting-Ray 52 · Hung-Spectrum-gallery 51 · Optician's-Plate 48 · Beamwalk 47 · Long-Gallery 45. The wild concepts (corridor renders only 1 of 11 readable; schematic can't carry 11 blurbs) genuinely failed the *compare-11-benches* test — exactly DESIGNING.md's "collections stay legible." (2) **My curatorial call** (owning the design per [[i-own-all-project-calls]]): take the judge's bulletproof Dispersion spine but graft the gallery concept's **live-vignette soul** so it's not "just a coloured stripe" — that graft is the heart, not chrome. (3) a **build deputy** evolved the file + browser-verified on a served origin: all 11 links + Ripple kin + back-link; Feats 0/3/9 contract (set 3 → 3/9 + right chips lit; set 9 → 9/9 + capstone hint); 380px reflow (scrollWidth===380, no overflow); console clean; **62fps**; contrast unchanged (name=`--ink`, blurb=`--ink-dim`, colour confined to rail/edges/prism/vignettes); `prefers-reduced-motion` honored. I then reviewed the actual screenshots myself (desktop/hover/mobile/feats-9of9) — the aesthetic call is the lead's.

> - **Seedbed:** the `[curation] Redesign the Hall` seed BLOOMED (pruned, with a follow-on note: apply the live-vignette/distinctive-form treatment to other *collection* rooms only where a metaphor is genuinely being wasted). Gauge → `fuel=8 · builds-since-plan=1`.

That's precisely the rut-breaker the planning system was built to be.

> **▶ THE PLANNING SYSTEM — a gardener/builder workflow so sessions sometimes shape *direction*, not just build the next exhibit (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Scaffolding shipped + Brandon's first seeds sown.** A strategic chat with Brandon, not a build. He named three cold-start limits of a context-bounded agent on a now-large project: (1) **metagames get forgotten** — new rooms don't tie into the Undercroft / Survey of Heaven; (2) **repetitive room design** — the Hall of Mirrors copied the front-door's vertical door-list and wasted an optics metaphor; (3) **no high-level plan** — sessions pick the nearest plausible exhibit and never step up to a new room/metagame/engine, or curate. Root cause (his): *I'm trained to follow the user's lead, not my own, and at scale no single context holds the whole estate.*
> - **The synthesis (all three are one root cause → one fix):** make the missing consideration *unavoidable at the moment of decision*, not "another doc to remember." A **gardener** sows; a **builder** grows. Where I pushed back on Brandon (the anti-sycophancy check — agreeing with everything would *be* the bug he named): I'd proposed a *forced forge check* for metagame integration; he rightly softened it (not everything needs every metagame; some are complete) → metagame health became a **curation axis + `kind: metagame` seeds**, with "complete — do not pad" a recordable state. Cadence: his RNG idea → my **stateful gauge** (RNG ignores the real signal); his flat idea-pool → my **two gauges** (exhibit *fuel* vs direction *bets*).
> - **Shipped (3 files):** **`ROADMAP.md`** — the seedbed: typed seeds (`exhibit·room·metagame·engine·curation·cross`) each **≤3 lines** (the "sow don't dictate" rule, structural), bootstrapped from the migrated NOTES next-steps menu + a metagame-health table. **`DESIGNING.md`** — the gardener's charter (survey/sow/curate-incl-**retire**/metagame-health/prune) + the builder's pipeline (**floor-not-ceiling**, **form-expresses-content** [strong-metaphor rooms diverge, collections stay legible], divergence-before-commit, metagame-as-consideration, the one `ws:seen` plumbing rule, definition-of-done). **`NOTES.md`** — a **🎲 mode gauge** section near the top (`PLAN if fuel ≲4 OR builds ≥4; else BUILD` ≈ 1-in-5, no RNG) + a live gauge line; migrated the inline next-steps menu + the stale "fresh thread" list into the seedbed.
> - **Mine, added unprompted:** a 4th observation — *the estate only ever grows; nothing is retired/merged/deepened* → **retire** is now an explicit gardener duty. > **▶ FEATS OF LIGHT — earned achievements on the Hall pieces, wired into THREE surfaces + a hidden capstone (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed.** Brandon, after the Hall shipped: *"move some to the Undercroft? or at least add earned badges — setting the perfect rainbow, centering the spyglass, solving the mirror maze… and put hooks into the Survey of Heaven too."* My call: **don't hide the public pieces** (the Hall is a complete public wing); instead make each bench a little **challenge with a goal**, and let mastery write to the metagame.
> - **The 9 feats** — each Hall piece now fires a raise-only `ws:flag:earned-<flag>` + a gilt "✦ Feat of Light earned" toast + a persistent topbar **goal chip**, ONLY on a genuine skill (edge-triggered, never trivially true, verified by negative tests): **Rainbow** land the impact-param on the Descartes min-deviation ray (b≈0.861, ~42°) · **Halo** the 60°-prism min-deviation ray (the 22° halo) · **Spyglass** bring M into the marked 8–12× focus band · **Camera Obscura** set the pinhole within 3% of d_opt · **Spectroscope** identify a line/source · **Polariser** rotate to full extinction · **Anamorphosis** align the reconstruction · **Iridescence** match a target colour · **Mirror Maze** a genuine PLAYER solve (not "show solution"). Flags: `earned-{rainbow,halo,spyglass,camera,spectroscope,polariser,anamorphosis,iridescence,maze}`.
> - **Surface 1 — the Hall ribbon:** `hall-of-mirrors/index.html` gained a "✦ Feats of Light — N/9 earned" ribbon (reads the flags; the on-ramp, per the hidden-features-need-an-on-ramp lesson) + a 9/9 hint that nudges toward the Undercroft without handing over the map.
> - **Surface 2 — the Survey of Heaven** (Brandon's ask): a new **feats-driven constellation "The Optician"** ("Bends every ray to its purpose.") — 9 stars in the west grounds by the Hall that kindle star-by-star as feats are earned and complete at 9/9 (brass line + engraved name + its own sub-tally + `ws:flag:sky-feats-named`). Done by extending `Sky.state` with an optional `feats` group + `visitedFromStore` mapping `ws:flag:earned-<X>`→`feat-<X>`, reusing the SAME asterism machinery as the 6 wings but **deliberately excluded from `allComplete`** so the original 6-wing capstone is structurally protected (verified both directions). Sky self-test **22/22 → 41/41**; 0 label collisions in the 9/9 state.
> - **Surface 3 — the hidden capstone (secret #12): "The Light Mixer"** (`undercroft/light-mixer.html`) — additive colour **synthesis**, the Spectroscope's exact inverse: a spinning **Newton's disc** that fuses to the area-weighted **linear-light** average (full spectrum → near-white/D65) + three **additive spotlights** (R+G=Y, G+B=C, B+R=M, R+G+B=white). Self-test **7/7** (sRGB⇄linear, disc==closed-form average, additive laws exact, radiometric not gamma-space). Unlocked by **all 9 feats** — predicate added to `tools/ws/ws.js` `WS.SECRETS` + a display row (riddle + 9 signs) in `undercroft/index.src.html`.
> - **Orchestration (ultracode):** Phase 1 = a **Workflow** (`hall-feats-of-light`) fanning out 9 worktree-isolated agents, each instrumenting one piece (shared earnFeat helper + the skill condition + goal chip) and **browser-verifying the feat actually FIRES** (drove the UI to the satisfying state, saw the toast + confirmed the localStorage flag) — staged to `/tmp/feats-of-light/`, lead copied onto main (purely additive, 531 ins). Phase 2 = 2 parallel deputies (Survey hook + the capstone piece) + lead wiring (the ribbon; `WS.SECRETS`; `forge --all` — editing the shared `ws.js` restales all 87 inlined pages). A final **end-to-end QA deputy** validated the whole chain on a **served origin** (localStorage is origin-keyed): locked baseline (capstone hidden) → 3 feats (3 stars kindle) → 9 feats (ribbon 9/9 + hint, "The Optician" complete, **The Light Mixer unlocked & loads 7/7**), original capstone unaffected, 0 overlaps, every piece still green. **PASS, no defects.**
> - **Lessons:** (1) the **shared agent-browser daemon tab gets hijacked** under heavy parallelism even with unique session names — the agents that succeeded used an **isolated headless Chrome via a private `--cdp`/CDP tab**; give heavy-parallel browser agents that isolation up front. (2) the first Workflow script failed to parse twice — **markdown ``` fences and over-escaped `\\'` inside a template-literal prompt**; build agent prompts as a `[...].join('\n')` array of plain strings, no backtick fences. (3) editing the inlined `tools/ws/ws.js` means `forge --all`, not a single-file forge.

> **▶ THE HALL OF MIRRORS — a new front-door OPTICS WING (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed.** Brandon's nudge ("a Hall of Mirrors — all things pertaining to light: prisms, mirrors, lenses, fargazers, diffusers") landed on a real gap: the estate had a *light thread* (Caustic the ray-bench, Kaleidoscope the mirror toy, Ripple the wave tank) but **no dedicated optics wing**, and several beautiful light phenomena weren't built. Treated the nudge as inspiration, not spec (my project, my calls) and built a coherent **gallery of light** — a new front-door ROOM that homes **8 self-contained benches**: 6 brand-new + Caustic & Kaleidoscope, both **promoted out of the now-overloaded Workbench** into their truer home.
> - **The six new pieces** (each a single zero-dep HTML file, dark-gilt estate aesthetic, a built-in self-test proving its physics EXACT, browser-verified — green chip, clean console — + a `CHANGELOG.md`):
>   - **The Rainbow** 🌈 `rainbow/` — derives the rainbow from one spherical droplet: refract in (Snell) → k internal reflections → refract out; the minimum-deviation (Descartes) ray piles light into the caustic bow. **Primary 42.00°, secondary 51.04°** (computed by numerically minimising `D_k(i)=2(i−r)+k(π−2r)`, *not* baked), Alexander's dark band, reversed secondary colours, per-λ dispersion (red 42.37° > violet 40.51°), supernumeraries. Magnified-drop view + antisolar sky view, true wavelength→sRGB. Self-test **9/9** (drop trace matches closed form, maxErr 0.0).
>   - **The Spyglass** 🔭 `spyglass/` — telescope ray bench ("fargazer"). Keplerian refractor (real inverted intermediate image, exit pupil) + Newtonian reflector (parabola). M = f_obj/f_eye **exact (err 0)**; lens eq err 8.7e-19; parabola focuses 24 parallel rays through (0,f) to **7.5e-14** = no spherical aberration, while a sphere misses its paraxial focus by **134.7px** (the contrast has teeth). 1× vs magnified+inverted scene. Self-test **9/9**.
>   - **The Spectroscope** 🌈 `spectroscope/` — prism (Cauchy, min-deviation) + diffraction grating (`d·sinθ=mλ`, orders ±1/±2), then real **line spectra** at true wavelength→sRGB: H Balmer, Na-D doublet, Hg, Ne, solar **Fraunhofer** absorption. Balmer Hα **computed 656.29 nm** (used the reduced-mass-corrected `R_H`, then compared the *air* wavelength — physically exact, lands the textbook value; R∞ alone misses by 0.19nm). Self-test **7/7**.
>   - **The Polariser** 🕶️ `polariser/` — Malus's law `I=I₀cos²θ` (full→dark crossed), the E-field projection drawn; the **three-polariser paradox** (crossed pair dark; insert a 45° middle → light returns, `¼·sin²2θ`, peak **¼ at 45°** to 1.67e-16). On-screen intensities routed through the *same proven core* as the self-test (no hard-coded values). Self-test **9/9**.
>   - **The Anamorphic Mirror** 🪞 `anamorphosis/` — cylindrical-mirror (catoptric) anamorphosis: a warped table-smear that a vertical cylinder reflects back upright. Exact invertible map (azimuth preserved; radius a Möbius map). Three live panels (source / warped table / mirror reconstruction). Round-trip `unwarp(warp(P))=P` err **1.2e-15**, horizontal-line→circle-arc 2.2e-16, reconstruction of every control point 6.9e-16. Self-test **6/6**.
>   - **Iridescence** 🫧 `iridescence/` — thin-film interference: Newton's rings (`t=r²/2R`, dark `2t=mλ`, `r_m=√(mRλ)`, dark centre), draining soap film, oil slick. **True perceived colour by integrating the reflected spectrum against the CIE-1931 CMFs (Wyman fit) under D65 → sRGB** — not a fake hue ramp. Self-test **9/9** (rings exact; flat spectrum → neutral x≈y≈⅓; perfect reflector → D65; ring radius ∝√λ). 61fps animated oil mode.
> - **Orchestration (the proven pattern, at scale):** scoped each piece with a complete self-contained spec, then dispatched **6 worktree-isolated background deputies in two waves of 3** (Brandon: "tons of quota — leverage subagents to extend your run"), each building ONE piece and self-verifying in a **uniquely-named** agent-browser session, committing to its branch, reporting only a short summary (guarding lead context). Integrated each new dir to `main` via `git -C $ROOT checkout <branch> -- <dir>` + commit (disjoint new dirs → zero conflicts). A **7th QA-sweep deputy** then verified the whole wing in one uncontended browser: map + Hall + all 6 pieces **PASS**, **0 label collisions** across all 11 room labels, all 9 hrefs **200**, every chip green, every console clean — verdict *ready to push*.
> - **The front door:** appended ONE `PLACES` entry (`id:hall-of-mirrors`, accent `#8fd9ff`, west grounds x124 y430, `prefer:bottom`) + wrote a **new `hall` footprint drawer** (`drawHall` in `index.src.html` — a long vaulted gallery, a colonnade of arched **windows** along the north wall facing arched **mirrors** (drawn with the lit lip so they catch the light) along the south, a centre ridge, a west doorway). Forged + `--check --all` green. The Hall sits between the Observatory and the Glasshouse (two light rooms — thematically apt).
> - **The Hall room** (`hall-of-mirrors/index.html`): a luminous Workbench-styled gallery (a faint spectral wash across the top, a prismatic title gradient) grouping the 8 benches as *Rays/lenses/mirrors* · *Colour & spectrum* · *The wave nature of light*, with a "kin elsewhere → **Ripple**" note. **Workbench edited:** Caustic + Kaleidoscope cards removed, a `↗ The Hall of Mirrors` pointer added; the map's Workbench blurb updated. README + NOTES + this worklog updated.
> - **Lessons reinforced:** (1) CWD drifts into a completed worktree when its notification arrives — `git -C $ROOT …` for *every* integration command (caught it on the first integration). (2) Even with **unique** agent-browser session names, parallel deputies can still collide on the shared daemon's tab (two reported it); workarounds that held — re-asserting the page with chained open+verify, or launching a dedicated headless Chrome via `--cdp`. Give heavy-parallel browser deputies an isolation hint next time.
> - **Bonus round (→ 10 benches):** the core 8-bench wing shipped + live (commit `7e94564`, all 7 URLs 200), then — quota to spare — a second 2-deputy wave rounded it out. **The Halo** ☀️ `halo/`: ice-crystal atmospheric optics, the Rainbow's *frozen twin* — **22°=21.76° / 46°=45.52° halos** + sundogs + circumzenithal arc (cutoff ≈32.4°) + parhelic circle, all from numerically minimising ice-prism deviation, red inner edge from n_ice(λ); 10/10. **The Camera Obscura** 📷 `camera-obscura/`: pinhole image formation — inverted, m=v/u exact, the energy-conserving blur convolution showing the sharp↔diffraction sweet spot. The deputy **improved on my spec**: I quoted the distant-scene optimal pinhole `√(2.44λv)`, but it derived the *exact* finite-distance optimum `d=√(2.44λ·v·u/(u+v))` (matches the numeric argmin to machine precision; the classic rule shown alongside as the rule-of-thumb); 10/10. Both browser-verified (tabs not hijacked — only 2 parallel), integrated, carded, README/NOTES/this-log updated.
> - **An 11th bench — a PLAY dimension:** **The Mirror Maze** 🪞 `mirror-maze/`, a playable, provably-solvable **laser-reflection puzzle** — a laser enters a grid, you place `/`\`\\` mirrors to route the beam through every gem. **Provably solvable by construction:** the generator walks a beam inward dropping mirrors, makes the crossed cells the targets, then **prunes the solution to minimal** so every shipped mirror is load-bearing (which also gives the win-check teeth and a tight token budget). The beam-tracer detects revisited `(cell,dir)` states + a step cap so it **always terminates**. Self-test **5/5**: all 8 reflection cases exact; tracer halts on adversarial looping boards; **240 seeded boards across sizes — every reference solution lights 100% of targets, 0 failures**; win-detection has teeth (remove/rotate one mirror → unsolved); seed-reproducible. It crosses the Hall (light/reflection) with the puzzle vein (Latch/Slitherlink/Akari) and is the most literal hall-of-mirrors piece — given its own **"A puzzle of light"** group in the gallery. **The wing now stands at 11 pieces** (10 study-benches + this puzzle).

> **▶ FIX — THE SURVEY OF HEAVEN read as a BUG: constellations now legible as named star-figures (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** Brandon (the site's owner) opened the live map and reported what looked like three defects: "The Scribe" and "The Gardener" labels each with **a line that points to nowhere**, and **unlabeled dots** scattered about — asking, reasonably, *bug or secret?* Diagnosis: **not a bug — the Survey of Heaven metagame working exactly as designed** (every room visited kindles a star; visiting both halves of a companion-wing completes its asterism → a brass line + an engraved name + myth; the corner tally read "2/6 skies charted" = The Scribe [verse+scriptorium] + The Gardener [strange-garden+tessellarium], and the dots were field stars for other visited rooms). **But the confusion is the finding:** the feature was *too* subtle to read as intentional — the engraved name sat in a margin berth **detached** from its constellation, and the two member stars were faint, so the name looked like it labelled nothing and the brass line looked like it pointed nowhere. The whole map ties every ROOM label to its room with a leader; the constellations alone had none (a prior deliberate "no leader, proximity only" call) — and that very inconsistency is what made them read as broken.
> - **The fix (legibility, no logic change):** (1) **charted stars** — the two member stars of a *completed* asterism are now drawn brighter/larger in brass with a stronger halo (`.sky-star.charted`, tagged in `tools/sky/sky.js` from the complete-asterism member set), so the line visibly terminates on two real stars. (2) **a faint dashed brass leader** ties each engraved name to its nearest charted star (`.an-leader`) — the *same* draughting idiom every POI label already uses — built in `buildSkyNames`/`applySkyName` (`index.src.html`) using the existing `LabelPlacer.geom.nearestEdgePoint`. (3) **stub suppression** — when the name already abuts its star (leader < 14px, e.g. The Scribe) no stub is drawn; proximity + the bright anchor-star carry the tie (avoids a glitchy 3px dash). Now a named sky reads as ONE unit: name → tie → two bright stars joined by a line.
> - **The metagame logic is untouched** — `tools/sky/sky.test.cjs` still **22/22**; only the rendering/CSS changed.
> - **Verified in a real browser** (served `:8731`, agent-browser session `skyqa`, viewport 1440×900 @2×): reproduced Brandon's exact **2/6** state (Scribe adjacent → no stub + bright stars; Gardener → visible ~30px leader), then the full **6/6** state — all six constellations (The Astronomer/Compositor/Maze & Thread/Cartographer/Scribe/Gardener) render as legible named figures with the all-skies capstone glow. **DOM bounding-box collision check: 6 sky names vs 40 POI labels → 0 collisions** (the two-pass solver still holds; POI layout byte-identical). `forge --check --all` green (29 files). NB during verify: python `http.server` serves HTML with no-cache absent → Chrome cached the old `index.html`; cache-bust with a `?v=N` query to see forge changes.
> - **The unlabeled dots are LEFT AS-IS by design** — they're field stars (rooms you've visited that haven't completed a wing), the metagame's quiet breadcrumbs; Brandon said he's happy to "play to discover," so the mystery stays. The fix only makes the *completed* figures read as intentional, not broken.

> **▶ BONUS — THE BLACK CHAMBER: a codebreaker's desk that cracks classical ciphers by analysis (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed; commit `1abd15e` (+ Workbench card & README bullet in the consolidation).** Completes the estate's cipher story — it had makers (Volvelle, Scytale, the hidden Enigma) but no BREAKER. Recovers plaintext from ciphertext with NO key, by classical cryptanalysis. `tools/cryptanalysis/cryptanalysis.js` (DOM-free, module-guarded, forge+Node, seeded): **Caesar** by χ² vs English letter freqs (exact); **Vigenère** by the **index of coincidence** to find the period, then per-column χ² (exact keyword); **monoalphabetic substitution** by a seeded hill-climb/anneal on a **trigram** log-fitness (a compact ~5KB embedded English corpus → a trigram table at load — no big data files). Self-test **14/14** Node, 5/5 in-page: Caesar **156/156 exact**; Vigenère **48/48 exact keyword** + period identified (IoC@6=0.0778 over its neighbours); substitution mean ~100% on corpus / ≥90% held-out; **IoC(English) 0.0689 vs IoC(random) 0.0385**; χ²(English) 33 ≪ χ²(wrong-shift) 9600; auto-detect classifies all three; determinism. Page: paste/sample ciphertext → auto-detect or pick → watch the letter-frequency chart vs English, the IoC, the period, and (for substitution) the plaintext resolve from gibberish to readable English live. Browser-verified (`:8151`, session `crypt-verify`): recovered keywords CIPHER/LEMON at 100%, substitution to 97.3%, auto-detect correct, 0 console errors. Honest caveats (SPEC): Caesar exact always; Vigenère needs adequate per-column ciphertext; substitution is statistical (~97–100% on ≳300 letters). 3 skins, 2× PNG. Workbench → Instruments. (The session's 12th piece — a capstone to the cipher vein: makers → breaker.)

> **▶ BONUS — THE STRAIGHTEDGE: a Peaucellier linkage that draws an exact straight line (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed; commit `8ecf9ae` (+ Workbench card & README bullet in the consolidation).** The estate's FIRST planar-linkage / kinematics piece. The hero is the **Peaucellier–Lipkin linkage** (1864) — the first mechanism to convert pure rotation into a MATHEMATICALLY EXACT straight line, by circle inversion: a rhombus (4 equal bars) + 2 equal long bars from a fixed pivot O make the pen P the circle-inverse of a point Q (`|OP|·|OQ| = L²−ℓ²` const); drive Q on a circle through O and P traces a perfect line. `tools/linkage/linkage.js` (DOM-free, module-guarded, forge+Node) solves the configuration per crank angle; ships Peaucellier + a four-bar crank-rocker (its curved coupler curve is the foil that shows why exact-straight is special). Self-test **14/14**: **Peaucellier max perpendicular deviation from a perfect line = 4.88e-15** (machine precision — exact, NOT approximate; the soul of the piece), the inversive invariant to ~9e-16, bar-length loop-closure to ~1e-15 (the linkage truly closes — no cheating the geometry), four-bar all-5-bar drift ~1e-15 + 0 dead spots, determinism + skin-invariance. Animated bars/pin-joints, crank + angle scrubber, live trace + a live `straight to ±…e-15` readout, 3 skins, 2× PNG. Browser-verified (`:8149`, session `linkage-verify`): the pen visibly draws a dead-straight line, changing lengths re-solves exact, 0 console errors. **Dropped the pantograph** (its exact-affine claim is provable but a rigid free-roaming parallelogram collapses to a degenerate stick on screen — the "smaller proven set" rule). Workbench → Toys & benches. (The session's 10th piece.)

Self-test **12/12** Node (5 core: (1) ideal EXACTLY binomial — Σ=1 to 2e-15, mean=Np, var=Npq to 7e-14, Pascal row 1·4·6·4·1/16; (2) ≥100k-ball seeded runs [fair AND biased] do-not-reject χ² at α=0.01 — AND a flat histogram IS rejected, so the test has POWER; + CDF calibrated to published χ² quantiles, err ≤5e-4; (3) every path is a valid lattice path, bin=#right ∈[0,N], conservation; (4) determinism; (5) empirical mean/var track Np/Npq; +7 more), 5/5 in-page. > **▶ BONUS — KALEIDOSCOPE: a live mirror-toy with provable dihedral symmetry (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed; commit `6bb4c81` (+ Workbench card & README bullet in the consolidation).** A tumbling chamber of translucent glass viewed through an N-fold mirror, exactly Dₙ-symmetric and animated. `tools/kaleido/kaleido.js` (DOM-free, module-guarded, forge+Node) folds each Dₙ-orbit (n rotations + n reflections) to one fundamental wedge `φ∈[0,π/n]`, so every pixel `f(P)=content(fold(P))` is symmetric BY CONSTRUCTION (an identity, not a fake rotate). Adjustable order 3–12, seeded tumbling shards, 3 palette-only skins, 2× PNG. Self-test **9/9** Node (4 core: Dₙ-invariance max err **~2e-14** — `content(fold(P))==content(fold(g·P))` ∀ g∈Dₙ; fold lands in + is idempotent on the wedge; determinism + skin-invariance; order sweep |Dₙ|=2n; + 5 hardening: exhaustive invariance, group structure, fold purity, total skin-invariance, finiteness), 4/4 in-page (same core). Browser-verified (`:8147`, session `kaleido-verify`): live field symmetry 1.4e-14 (D6) / 1.5e-14 (D10), order re-symmetrizes live, reseed changes contents, skins geometry-identical, reduced-motion guard, 0 console errors. **Dedupe (grepped + NOTES inventory) confirmed distinct:** Rosette = a *static* rose window; Tessellarium = *translational* wallpaper symmetry (17 plane groups); this = live POINT-dihedral, adjustable, tumbling. Workbench → Toys & benches. (The session's 8th piece — a lean, silent overnight bonus.)

> **▶ BONUS — THE PATIENCE ENGINE: a solitaire whose dealer only deals provably-winnable hands (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed after the big wave; commit `4bd9b13` (+ the Workbench card & README bullet in the consolidation).** A new genre for the estate (it had zero card pieces). The Lantern idea, dealt onto cards: a headless SOLVER searches a dealt game for a win, and the DEALER only ships deals it has already beaten (rejection sampling) — so "a deal you can't lose" is true by construction AND proven by the self-test. `tools/patience/patience.js` (DOM-free, module-guarded, forge+Node) is variant-as-data: a **compact FreeCell** (28-card A–7×4, 6 columns, 3 free cells) chosen so the reachable graph stays small and the solver beats essentially every board in a few thousand nodes (a 40-deal proving battery runs <0.4s); the *same* engine also deals/solves full 52-card FreeCell as a generality check. Solver = deterministic **weighted-A\*** (`f=g+3h`, foundation-distance `h`) + a transposition table + cycle avoidance + FreeCell safe-automove pruning + a 200k-node budget; the cached winning line drives `hint()` + watch-it-solve. Self-test **11/11** Node (6 core: solver soundness — every returned line is legal move-by-move and reaches the win; **winnability guarantee** — a seed battery all solve AND replay to a win; determinism; move-gen correctness; 28-card conservation; +5 cross-cutting), 6/6 in-page (same core). Page: a dark-felt **click-to-move** FreeCell — Deal-(guaranteed-winnable) w/ seed, Hint (pulse the next move), Undo, Watch-it-solve (replays the proven line to the foundations), illegal moves declined, win celebration. Forge page using WS (`ws:seen:patience`). Browser-verified (served `:8146`, session `patience-verify`, desktop + mobile), 0 console errors. Honest scope (in SPEC §0): every *dealt* game is winnable — not every conceivable deal, and not that you'll win (you can still strand yourself; Undo/Hint are there). Build gotcha fixed: `forge:include` directives must sit INSIDE an open `<script>` block. Workbench → "Games of perfect information". Picked as the "one more cool thing" after the wave (the ideation's runner-up engine).

> **▶ THE BIG CREATIVE WAVE — a new ENGINE, a new METAGAME, and three growth POIs, built by a fan-out of parallel deputies (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"; Brandon away overnight). Five pieces shipped & pushed; HEAD `dbaf864`; tree clean, synced, `forge --check --all` green, no worktrees.** Commits: `956b92f` (Survey) · `5e260d2` (Adversary) · `41a360f` (Singing Plate) · `f9c2db3` (Bulwark + arcade tag bump) · `d9beeaa` (Gnomon) · `dbaf864` (Workbench cards + README consolidation). Process docs: `f9ff0a6`/`79ef61c` (the durable `worklog/big-wave-plan.md` plan + ledger).
> 
> **(0) Ideation as a fan-out (honoring Brandon's "explore outward like fractals, curate their discoveries").** Ran a Workflow (`wf_046852b2-a3c`): **11 idea SCOUTS** (4 engine angles, 4 metagame angles, 3 POI scouts) each repo-grounded (read NOTES + the hidden inventory + Lantern/WS/forge) and returning a structured proposal (concept, novelty/dedupe-check, the provable claim, forge-share story, map-surface, complexity, risks, a build-ready spec) → **1 CURATOR** that ranked per thread, dedupe-checked vs the existing/hidden inventory (greps confirmed every proposed `tools/` dir absent), and produced a build order + contention strategy. I saved the full curation to a durable `worklog/big-wave-plan.md` (so the overnight run survived `/tmp` eviction + context resets), then dispatched build deputies. PICKS: **The Adversary** (engine), **The Survey of Heaven** (metagame), and POIs **The Singing Plate / The Gnomon / Bulwark**. Runner-ups recorded for later: The Patience Engine, a logic-puzzle generator, "The Hours" (real-time living estate).
> 
> **(1) ♟️ The Adversary** (`adversary/` + `tools/game/`, `5e260d2`) — a reusable **combinatorial-game-theory engine** and a new GENRE for the estate (a thinking, unbeatable adversary). `tools/game/adversary.js` (DOM-free, module-guarded for forge+Node) ENUMERATES a game's reachable state graph (BFS over a symmetry-canonical key, hard-capped) then RETROGRADE-LABELS every position WIN/LOSS/DRAW with exact distance-to-end by backward induction — and the SAME solve table drives both play and the self-test, so the opponent and the proof can't drift. Lantern-shaped: 5 games supplied as pure data (`tools/game/games/{nim,ttt333,konane,hex3,mnk443}.js`), each proven — Nim [3,4,5] WIN mate-11, ttt333 DRAW, 4×4 Kōnane LOSS mate-6, Hex-3 WIN mate-5, (3,4,3) m,n,k WIN mate-7. Players: perfectPlayer / randomPlayer(seed) / describeForAgent + an `llmPlayer` stub (the Lantern bot-hook parallel). Page: play vs perfection, "reveal verdicts" tints every legal move WIN/LOSS/DRAW + "mate in N", Hint, watch-perfect-vs-perfect (halts at the proven outcome), honest "you let a forced win slip", 2× PNG. Self-test **38/38** Node (literature values incl. the Nim XOR theorem + ttt=DRAW; table self-consistency; optimal-vs-optimal outcome+distance; **perfect-never-loses exhaustive sweep**; symmetry-canon soundness; node-budget), 30/30 in-page. **Design call:** m,n,k shipped as (3,4,3) — the 4×4·3 graph (300,001 nodes) exceeds the 300k cap + the <1s in-browser budget; per the "smaller proven set beats five with a bug" rule the genre + proof were kept. The self-test EARNED ITS KEEP — caught 4 real bugs mid-build (a perfectPlayer canonical-vs-live-move mismatch, an order-dependent retrograde distance, a `playFrom` POV parity bug, a painter arg-swap).
> 
> **(2) ✶ The Survey of Heaven** (`tools/sky/` + `index.src.html`, `956b92f`) — the **cross-page metagame**, whose visible surface IS the front-door map. It READS the `ws:seen:<id>` breadcrumb EVERY page already drops (so zero per-page instrumentation; touches only `index.src.html` + 3 new `tools/sky/` files): first visit to a room kindles a star over its quarter of the dark plate; the six real **companion-wings** (celestial/design/labyrinth/realm/letters/garden) join into asterisms that COMPLETE when both members are seen — lines brighten to brass, an engraved name (The Astronomer, The Cartographer, …) is typeset in the margin; charting all six fires an all-skies capstone; a "Survey of Heaven — N/6 charted" tally is the on-ramp. `tools/sky/sky.js` (Sky global, forge-inlined + Node-testable) is a pure core + a thin renderer; writes only NEW cosmetic namespaced flags (`ws:flag:sky-*`, `ws:flag:firmament-survey`) — no existing predicate touched, NO new secret, no `ws.js` edit; `Sky.bootstrap` silences retroactive name-animations for returning visitors. **The integration crux:** adding the sky's names to the map's single shared label-solve perturbed the seeded annealing and shifted an unrelated POI label onto a footprint → fixed by a **SECOND, independent label-solve pass** (POI labels solve first; asterism names then solve against those rects + footprints + furniture as obstacles), so the POI layout is **byte-identical to baseline regardless of sky state** and DOM-truth overlaps stay 0 in all states. Self-test **22/22** (determinism/order-independence, **monotonicity**, **completion-iff** over a member-minus-one battery, catalog stars all inside the viewBox & off every footprint, bijection). Undercroft gating, hover cards, avenues, a11y, reduced-motion, storage-off degradation, and the forget-reset all preserved.
> 
> **(3) 🎛️ The Singing Plate** (`singing-plate/` + `tools/plate/`, `41a360f`) — a **Chladni vibration bench** and the workshop's FIRST spectral solver; the third panel of the wave-physics trilogy (Caustic the rays, Ripple the travelling waves, the Plate the standing ones). `tools/plate/plate.js` discretizes the negative-Laplacian −Δ on a masked shape (square / inscribed disk; clamped/free) and numerically SOLVES the eigenproblem −Δu=λu with a from-scratch, deterministic, seeded **Rayleigh-Ritz Lanczos** eigensolver (dense projected H = QᵀLQ + cyclic Jacobi; no deps — full reorthogonalization breaks the three-term recurrence, so H is formed explicitly). Seeded sand drifts off antinodes onto the solved **nodal lines** (`xy += −η·∇(u²) + jitter·|u|`). Self-test **18/18** Node, 8/8 in-page: square eigenvalues CONVERGE to π²·{2,5,8,10,13} as the grid refines, circle eigenvalue RATIOS match Bessel-zero ratios `(j₁,₁/j₀,₁)²`, eigenvectors orthonormal (Gram≈I to 1e-15) + operator symmetric (max|L−Lᵀ|=0), sand-on-nodes ≥3×, determinism + skin-invariance. Membrane drumhead ships as the proven default; the 4th-order biharmonic plate is a clearly-labelled experimental stretch mode. **Distinct** from the watch-only `strange-garden/pieces/chladni.html` (a hardcoded closed-form animation, no eigensolver). No audio. Workbench → Toys & benches.
> 
> **(4) 🛡️ Bulwark** (`arcade/games/bulwark.*`, `f9c2db3`) — a neon **Defender/Scramble side-scroller** (arcade #19, the last classic-genre gap), shipping BOTH loops: the Defender rescue chain (a Lantern grabs a grounded tender, hauls it up; shoot it [+75] to free it; catch the falling tender [+250] to re-ground; a tender reaching the top mutates its Lantern into a faster strafer) AND the Scramble fuel loop (fuel burns, bomb a depot [+60] to refuel, terrain-crash/fuel-out = death). Full-ring scanner strip, thrust/inertia, mobile touch controls, SFX **muted by default** (odd-hours courtesy). Deterministic CORE (`arcade/games/bulwark.core.js`, module-guarded): seeded mulberry32 + a fixed-timestep accumulator → integer ticks (no `Math.random`, no wall-clock dt). Self-test 6 core + 6 harness: **replay determinism** (seed + scripted input → identical 900-tick state hash twice), horizontal-wrap continuity (shortest-arc delta), the rescue invariant (+75/+250 exact), fuel monotonicity, collision symmetry across the seam, seed-purity. I applied the front-door Arcade `tag` bump 18→19 + the blurb myself.
> 
> **(5) 🌅 The Gnomon** (`sundial/` + `tools/dial/`, `d9beeaa`) — an operable **sundial** whose shadow tells true clock time, the celestial sub-wing's iconic missing member. `tools/dial/dial.js` COPIES the Astrolabe's frozen solar functions verbatim (so the two instruments demonstrably read ONE sky) + adds hour-angle, the equation-of-time, per-dial hour-line angles, the gnomon style angle, and the cast-shadow projection. Three dials (horizontal/equatorial/vertical-south); set lat/lon/tz/date/time → the shadow sweeps; toggle the EoT correction → the civil readout matches the wall clock; "plot a year of noons" draws the **analemma** figure-8; 3 skins. Self-test **21/21** (round-trip clock over 12,250 states, shadow-tip on the closed-form hour-line <1e-5·r for all 3 types, hour-line angles == closed form [equatorial exactly 15°/hr], EoT extrema +16.45m≈Nov 3 / −14.20m≈Feb 11 + the four zero-crossings, gnomon angle == lat/co-lat, determinism + skin-invariance). Workbench → Instruments.
> 
> **(6) Orchestration + lessons (durable).** Pattern: ideation Workflow → curate → 5 **parallel background worktree deputies** (each built one piece + self-verified in a real browser on its OWN port [8141–8145] + a UNIQUELY-NAMED agent-browser session) → I reviewed screenshots, integrated each to `main`, pushed, retired the worktree, kept the pipeline ~3 deep. **LESSONS:** (a) the shell **CWD silently drifts into a completed worktree** when its task-notification arrives — a plain `git checkout <branch> -- <paths>` then no-op'd against the worktree itself and reported the worktree's already-clean state; fix = ALWAYS `git -C <root> checkout … -- <paths>` + absolute `node` paths + verify with `git -C <root> diff --cached --stat` before committing. (b) forge resolves includes relative to the `.src.html` dir and writes beside it → **absolute paths make forge CWD-independent** (`node <root>/tools/forge/forge.mjs <root>/x.src.html`; `--check --all <root>`). (c) **Lead does ALL shared-file edits** (the `workbench/index.html` cards, README, the map tag-bump) while deputies create ONLY new files → zero merge races on the hot files; the only two `index.src.html` writers (Survey's surface, Bulwark's tag) were serialized. A 10-min heartbeat cron backstopped the unattended run against accidental turn-ends.

> **▶ THE LETTERER — a point-feature label-placement engine — and the SELF-LETTERING MAP (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Brandon saw the live map and flagged overlapping POI labels (Gardens↔the Workshop cartouche, Undercroft↔Music Room, the Manor-House caption↔Print Room); his steer: *"build a label placement algorithm specimen … and use it for your map too."* So I built a real algorithm, proved it, shipped it as a specimen, then made the front door consume it. All pushed & live; HEAD `9a3a062`; tree clean, synced, `forge --check --all` green, no worktrees.** Commits: `b396acc` (the Letterer engine + specimen) · `9a3a062` (map self-letters).
> 
> **(1) The engine — `tools/label/label.js`.** Cartographic **Point-Feature Label Placement** (PFLP), a real NP-hard problem: drop a name beside every point so no two labels collide, none crosses an obstacle, all stay in-frame, each prefers its ideal side. Implements the classic **candidate-slot (fixed-position) model + simulated annealing** (Christensen, Marks & Shieber, ACM TOG 1995): for each feature, 4 or 8 candidate label-rects around the anchor; energy = Σ(label-label overlap·W + label-obstacle overlap·W + out-of-bounds·W + side-preference·W + leader-length·W) with overlaps dominating; SA with a **seeded PRNG (mulberry32) → fully deterministic**, geometric cooling, then a greedy local-improvement sweep; honours `pin`ned features (fixed, still act as obstacles). API `LabelPlacer.solve({bounds, features:[{id,anchor,label:{w,h},prefer,pin,gap}], obstacles, seed, positions, iterations}) → {placements:[{id,x,y,side,label,leader,cost,overlapped}], overlaps, energy}`. Module-guarded (braced single line so forge strips it) + CommonJS-exported + `root.LabelPlacer` for browsers — same dual-target pattern as `tools/ws/ws.js`. Node self-test `tools/label/label.test.cjs` **12/12**: determinism (byte-identical across runs), bounds containment, a **24-case feasible battery → overlaps===0** (0 label-label AND 0 label-obstacle), monotone SA improvement, pin honoured + avoided, graceful infeasibility (over-dense → no crash, bounded, residual reported), leader correctness.
> 
> **(2) The specimen — "The Letterer"** (`letterer/`, Workbench → Toys & benches, glyph ✍️). A live, beautiful demonstrator on the dark drafting aesthetic: scatter N named survey stations, hit **Anneal**, and watch the clashing cloud of overlapping names *cool* into a clean layout as the **overlap counter falls to 0** (phase pill → "settled · 0 overlaps"). Controls: reseed, station-count slider, click-to-add a station, **4↔8-position** toggle, **obstacles** toggle (a river + lake drawn as no-go bands the labels route around), candidate-slot reveal for one selected point, 4 anneal speeds (instant = the real `solve()`). The workshop signature, on-page: a self-test chip **`SELF-TEST 24/24 ✓ — 0 overlaps across 24 seeded layouts`**. Forge page (`<!-- forge:include tools/ws/ws.js -->` + `<!-- forge:include tools/label/label.js -->`), `ws:seen:letterer` breadcrumb, `← workshop` back-link, PNG 2×. Browser-verified on a real origin (`:8131`, session `letterer-verify`): anneal 9→0, counter→0, all controls, self-test green, PNG valid, **0 console errors**, handsome at 1440×900 + mobile 390×844 (fixed a mobile topbar wrap).
> 
> **(3) The map now letters itself** (`index.src.html`). The front door inlines `tools/label/label.js` via forge and computes its own label layout at load — **two-pass**: build + measure each label group (`getBBox`, +3px pad for the text's dark backing stroke) → `LabelPlacer.solve` with one feature per *present* POI (anchor = footprint centre; per-feature `gap` = footprint half-extent + margin so every candidate slot clears the building it names; `prefer` **seeded from the old curated side** so the hand-tuned composition is the start-point and only colliding labels move; `pin` wired through) → translate each label group onto its solved rect, set text-anchor from the chosen side, redraw a straight leader from the footprint boundary to the label's nearest edge. Obstacles = all footprints + compass rose + scale bar + title cartouche + both zone captions; bounds = inset frame. **Fixed seed `0x5EED` → byte-identical layout every load** (verified across 3 reloads — no jitter). Solved ONCE (the viewBox scales uniformly, so positions are resolution-independent — no re-solve on resize). Design call: the manor *shell* is deliberately NOT an obstacle (the 4 wing footprints inside it already guard the interior; walling the shell would trap the wings' own labels inside the house). **DOM-truth overlaps = 0** in both the fresh AND the Undercroft-revealed states (label↔label, label↔footprint, label↔furniture, out-of-bounds all clear); the three named collisions confirmed gone; the Undercroft gating (broken→rune→whole, grow/repair, margin rune, storage-off degradation), hover cards, accent glow, "↳ within" pills, survey avenues, a11y, reduced-motion all preserved; rune-click reuses the already-solved cellar-stair slot (no layout shift). **`lx/ly` are now OPTIONAL hints** — appending a future POI needs only a coordinate + footprint (+ optional `prefer`/`pin`). Browser-verified (`:8132`, session `map-relabel`) fresh + revealed + mobile, 0 console errors.
> 
> **(4) Orchestration.** Two sequential worktree deputies — #1 built the engine+specimen+test (no map changes), #2 (branched after #1 landed) wired the map; I reviewed screenshots between and integrated each via `git checkout <branch> -- <files>` + a fresh commit (the reliable path for the forge-generated `index.html`), pushed, retired the worktree. The map's cleanup *became* a live demonstration of a new provable algorithm — strictly better than nudging coordinates, and exactly the steer Brandon gave.
